Every table's schema lives here, so init_db() builds them all.\"\"\"\n     2\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     3\t\n     4\timport sqlite3\n     5\tfrom collections.abc import Iterator\n     6\tfrom contextlib import contextmanager\n     7\t\n     8\tfrom app import settings\n     9\t\n    10\tSCHEMA = \"\"\"\n    11\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    12\t    id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    13\t    email         TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n    14\t    name          TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    15\t    role          TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    16\t    password_hash TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    17\t    is_active     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    18\t    created_at    TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n    19\t);\n    20\t\"\"\"\n    21\t\n    22\t\n    23\t@contextmanager\n    24\tdef connect() -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    25\t    \"\"\"Open the database named by settings (read at call time, so tests can swap\n    26\t    it). Commits on success, rolls back on error, always closes.\"\"\"\n    27\t    conn = sqlite3.connect(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    28\t    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    29\t    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n    30\t    try:\n    31\t        with conn:\n    32\t            yield conn\n    33\t    finally:\n    34\t        conn.close()\n    35\t\n    36\t\n    37\tdef init_db() -> None:\n    38\t    with connect() as conn:\n    39\t        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n=== app/auth.py\n     1\t\"\"\"Sessions and CSRF.\n     2\t\n     3\tThe session is a signed cookie (Starlette's SessionMiddleware, which signs with\n     4\titsdangerous). It holds only the user's id and a CSRF token. The user is\n     5\tre-loaded from the database on every protected request, so deactivating an\n     6\taccount ends its sessions at once.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     9\t\n    10\timport secrets\n    11\timport sqlite3\n    12\t\n    13\tfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\n    14\t\n    15\tfrom app import users\n    16\t\n    17\t\n    18\tclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    19\t    \"\"\"Raised by current_user; main.py turns it into a redirect to /login.\"\"\"\n    20\t\n    21\t\n    22\tdef current_user(request: Request) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    23\t    \"\"\"Dependency: the logged-in, still-active user, or LoginRequired.\"\"\"\n    24\t    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    25\t    user = users.get_active_user(user_id) if user_id is not None else None\n    26\t    if user is None:\n    27\t        request.session.clear()\n    28\t        raise LoginRequired\n    29\t    return user\n    30\t\n    31\t\n    32\tdef require_director(user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(current_user)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    33\t    \"\"\"Dependency: current_user, but 403 unless they are a Director.\"\"\"\n    34\t    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n    35\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Directors only.\")\n    36\t    return user\n    37\t\n    38\t\n    39\tdef log_in(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    40\t    # A fresh session (and CSRF token) on login, so a pre-login cookie planted\n    41\t    # by someone else never becomes an authenticated one.\n    42\t    request.session.clear()\n    43\t    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n    44\t    csrf_token(request)\n    45\t\n    46\t\n    47\tdef log_out(request: Request) -> None:\n    48\t    request.session.clear()\n    49\t\n    50\t\n    51\tdef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    52\t    \"\"\"The session's CSRF token, created on first use. Templates call this.\"\"\"\n    53\t    token = request.session.get(\"csrf_token\")\n    54\t    if token is None:\n    55\t        token = request.session[\"csrf_token\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    56\t    return token\n    57\t\n    58\t\n    59\tasync def require_csrf(request: Request) -> None:\n    60\t    \"\"\"Dependency for every state-changing POST: the form's csrf_token must\n    61\t    match the session's.\"\"\"\n    62\t    form = await request.form()\n    63\t    submitted = form.get(\"csrf_token\")\n    64\t    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf_token\")\n    65\t    if not (isinstance(submitted, str) and expected\n    66\t            and secrets.compare_digest(submitted, expected)):\n    67\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")\n=== app/users.py\n     1\t\"\"\"User accounts: creation, lookup, and password checks.\n     2\t\n     3\tRoles are stored as `admin` / `editor` and shown as Director / Analyst.\n     4\tPasswords only ever exist here as argon2 hashes.\n     5\t\"\"\"\n     6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     7\t\n     8\timport re\n     9\timport sqlite3\n    10\t\n    11\tfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\n    12\tfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerificationError\n    13\t\n    14\tfrom app import db\n    15\t\n    16\tROLE_LABELS = {\"admin\": \"Director\", \"editor\": \"Analyst\"}\n    17\tMIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH = 8\n    18\t_EMAIL_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"[^@\\s]+@[^@\\s]+\\.[^@\\s]+\")\n    19\t\n    20\t_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n    21\t# Verified against when the email is unknown, so a miss costs the same time as\n    22\t# a wrong password and response timing does not reveal which accounts exist.\n    23\t_DUMMY_HASH = _hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\")\n    24\t\n    25\t\n    26\tclass UserError(ValueError):\n    27\t    \"\"\"A user-management request broke a rule; the message is safe to show.\"\"\"\n    28\t\n    29\t\n    30\tclass DuplicateEmail(UserError):\n    31\t    pass\n    32\t\n    33\t\n    34\tdef _check_role(role: str) -> None:\n    35\t    if role not in ROLE_LABELS:\n    36\t        raise UserError(f\"Choose a role: {' or '.join(ROLE_LABELS.values())}.\")\n    37\t\n    38\t\n    39\tdef create_user(email: str, password: str, role: str, name: str | None = None) -> int:\n    40\t    \"\"\"Create an active user. `name` defaults to the email's local part.\"\"\"\n    41\t    email = email.strip()\n    42\t    if not _EMAIL_PATTERN.fullmatch(email):\n    43\t        raise UserError(\"Enter a valid email address.\")\n    44\t    if name is not None and not name.strip():\n    45\t        raise UserError(\"Enter a name.\")\n    46\t    if len(password) < MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH:\n    47\t        raise UserError(f\"Passwords must be at least {MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH} characters.\")\n    48\t    _check_role(role)\n    49\t    try:\n    50\t        with db.connect() as conn:\n    51\t            cursor = conn.execute(\n    52\t                \"INSERT INTO users (email, name, role, password_hash) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n    53\t                (email, (name or email.split(\"@\")[0]).strip(), role,\n    54\t                 _hasher.hash(password)),\n    55\t            )\n    56\t    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n    57\t        raise DuplicateEmail(f\"{email} already has an account.\") from None\n    58\t    return cursor.lastrowid\n    59\t\n    60\t\n    61\tdef set_role(user_id: int, role: str) -> bool:\n    62\t    \"\"\"Change a user's role. False if there is no such user. Takes effect on the\n    63\t    user's next request, since sessions re-load the user every time.\"\"\"\n    64\t    _check_role(role)\n    65\t    with db.connect() as conn:\n    66\t        return conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET role = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n    67\t                            (role, user_id)).rowcount == 1\n    68\t\n    69\t\n    70\tdef deactivate(user_id: int) -> bool:\n    71\t    \"\"\"Block a user from logging in and end their open sessions. The row is\n    72\t    kept so their content stays attributed. False if there is no such user.\"\"\"\n    73\t    with db.connect() as conn:\n    74\t        return conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET is_active = 0 WHERE id = ?\",\n    75\t                            (user_id,)).rowcount == 1\n    76\t\n    77\t\n    78\tdef list_users() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    79\t    with db.connect() as conn:\n    80\t        return conn.execute(\n    81\t            \"SELECT id, email, name, role, is_active FROM users\"\n    82\t            \" ORDER BY is_active DESC, name COLLATE NOCASE\"\n    83\t        ).fetchall()\n    84\t\n    85\t\n    86\tdef get_active_user(user_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    87\t    with db.connect() as conn:\n    88\t        return conn.execute(\n    89\t            \"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ? AND is_active = 1\", (user_id,)\n    90\t        ).fetchone()\n    91\t\n    92\t\n    93\tdef authenticate(email: str, password: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    94\t    \"\"\"Return the active user with these credentials, or None — never says why.\"\"\"\n    95\t    with db.connect() as conn:\n    96\t        user = conn.execute(\n    97\t            \"SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?\", (email.strip(),)\n    98\t        ).fetchone()\n    99\t    try:\n   100\t        _hasher.verify(user[\"password_hash\"] if user else _DUMMY_HASH, password)\n   101\t    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):\n   102\t        return None\n   103\t    if user is None or not user[\"is_active\"]:\n   104\t        return None\n   105\t    if _hasher.check_needs_rehash(user[\"password_hash\"]):\n   106\t        with db.connect() as conn:\n   107\t            conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET password_hash = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n   108\t                         (_hasher.hash(password), user[\"id\"]))\n   109\t    return user\n=== app/main.py\n     1\t\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n     2\t\n     3\tThe admin console lives under /admin (login required) and the public preview\n     4\tat /. Routes live in their own modules under app/routes/ and are included here.\n     5\tKeep this file small.\n     6\t\"\"\"\n     7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     8\t\n     9\timport sys\n    10\tfrom contextlib import asynccontextmanager\n    11\t\n    12\tfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, HTTPException, Request\n    13\tfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n    14\tfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n    15\t\n    16\tfrom app import db, settings\n    17\tfrom app.auth import LoginRequired, current_user\n    18\tfrom app.routes import admin, auth, users\n    19\tfrom app.templating import templates\n    20\t\n    21\tSESSION_MAX_AGE = 8 * 60 * 60  # one working day\n    22\tALL_METHODS = [\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"PATCH\", \"DELETE\", \"OPTIONS\"]\n    23\t\n    24\t\n    25\t@asynccontextmanager\n    26\tasync def lifespan(app: FastAPI):\n    27\t    # At server start, not import, so importing app.main never touches a database.\n    28\t    db.init_db()\n    29\t    if settings.SECRET_KEY_IS_PLACEHOLDER:\n    30\t        print(\"WARNING: CMS_SECRET_KEY is unset or the .env.example placeholder; \"\n    31\t              \"session cookies can be forged. Set a long random value in .env.\",\n    32\t              file=sys.stderr)\n    33\t    yield\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tdef create_app() -> FastAPI:\n    37\t    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\", lifespan=lifespan)\n    38\t    app.add_middleware(\n    39\t        SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n    40\t        session_cookie=\"cms_session\", max_age=SESSION_MAX_AGE, same_site=\"lax\",\n    41\t    )\n    42\t\n    43\t    @app.exception_handler(LoginRequired)\n    44\t    def redirect_to_login(request: Request, exc: LoginRequired):\n    45\t        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n    46\t\n    47\t    @app.get(\"/\")\n    48\t    def public_home(request: Request):\n    49\t        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n    50\t            request, \"public/home.html\",\n    51\t            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n    52\t        )\n    53\t\n    54\t    app.include_router(auth.router)\n    55\t    app.include_router(admin.router)\n    56\t    app.include_router(users.router)\n    57\t\n    58\t    # Must stay last: any /admin request no router above claimed, by path or by\n    59\t    # method. Anonymous visitors are sent to login (so they can't probe which\n    60\t    # admin paths exist); signed-in users get 405/404.\n    61\t    @app.api_route(\"/admin\", methods=ALL_METHODS,\n    62\t                   dependencies=[Depends(current_user)], include_in_schema=False)\n    63\t    def admin_method_not_allowed():\n    64\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=405)\n    65\t\n    66\t    @app.api_route(\"/admin/{rest:path}\", methods=ALL_METHODS,\n    67\t                   dependencies=[Depends(current_user)], include_in_schema=False)\n    68\t    def admin_not_found(rest: str):\n    69\t        if rest == \"\":\n    70\t            return RedirectResponse(\"/admin\", status_code=307)\n    71\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n    72\t\n    73\t    return app\n    74\t\n    75\t\n    76\tapp = create_app()\n=== app/templating.py\n     1\t\"\"\"The Jinja environment the admin console renders with.\"\"\"\n     2\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     3\t\n     4\tfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n     5\t\n     6\tfrom app import settings\n     7\tfrom app.auth import csrf_token\n     8\tfrom app.users import ROLE_LABELS\n     9\t\n    10\ttemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n    11\ttemplates.env.globals[\"csrf_token\"] = csrf_token\n    12\ttemplates.env.filters[\"role_label\"] = ROLE_LABELS.get\n=== app/publish.py\n     1\t\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n     2\t\n     3\tT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\n     4\trender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n     5\t\n     6\t  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n     7\t  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     8\t     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n     9\t\"\"\"\n    10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n    11\t\n    12\timport shutil\n    13\tfrom pathlib import Path\n    14\t\n    15\tfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n    16\t\n    17\tfrom app import settings\n    18\t\n    19\tCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n    20\t:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\n    21\tbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\n    22\theader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\n    23\tmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n    24\t\"\"\"\n    25\t\n    26\t\n    27\tdef environment() -> Environment:\n    28\t    return Environment(\n    29\t        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n    30\t        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    31\t    )\n    32\t\n    33\t\n    34\tdef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    35\t    out = out or settings.SITE\n    36\t    if out.exists():\n    37\t        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    38\t    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    39\t    env = environment()\n    40\t    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    41\t    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n    42\t        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n    43\t            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n    44\t            home_path=\"index.html\",\n    45\t        )\n    46\t    )\n    47\t    return out\n=== app/settings.py\n     1\t\"\"\"Configuration, read from the environment (never hard-code secrets).\"\"\"\n     2\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     3\t\n     4\timport os\n     5\tfrom pathlib import Path\n     6\t\n     7\tROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\n     8\t\n     9\t\n    10\tdef _load_dotenv(path: Path) -> None:\n    11\t    \"\"\"Read KEY=VALUE lines from .env into os.environ. Variables already set in\n    12\t    the real environment win.\"\"\"\n    13\t    if not path.is_file():\n    14\t        return\n    15\t    for line in path.read_text().splitlines():\n    16\t        key, sep, value = line.partition(\"=\")\n    17\t        key = key.strip()\n    18\t        if not sep or not key or key.startswith(\"#\"):\n    19\t            continue\n    20\t        os.environ.setdefault(key, value.strip().strip(\"'\\\"\"))\n    21\t\n    22\t\n    23\t_load_dotenv(ROOT / \".env\")\n    24\t\n    25\tTEMPLATES = ROOT / \"templates\"\n    26\tSITE = ROOT / \"site\"\n    27\t\n    28\tSECRET_KEY = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SECRET_KEY\", \"dev-only-not-for-production\")\n    29\tSECRET_KEY_IS_PLACEHOLDER = SECRET_KEY in (\n    30\t    \"dev-only-not-for-production\", \"change-me-to-a-long-random-string\")\n    31\t# A relative CMS_DATABASE is relative to the repo, not the current directory.\n    32\tDATABASE_PATH = ROOT / os.environ.get(\"CMS_DATABASE\", \"cms.db\")\n    33\tSITE_TITLE = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SITE_TITLE\", \"My CMS\")\n    34\t# Set this to your Pages URL once you deploy, e.g.\n    35\t# https://yourname.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\n    36\tBASE_PATH = os.environ.get(\"CMS_BASE_PATH\", \"\")\n=== app/routes/admin.py\n     1\t\"\"\"The admin console.
